<script>

var hdscling = false;
window.addEventListener('scroll',function(){
    if(hdscling){
        return false;
    }
    hdscling = true;

    setTimeout(function(){
        let hd = document.querySelector('#header-2308');
        if( window.scrollY > 0 ){
            hd.classList.add('top0over');
        }else{
            hd.classList.remove('top0over');
        }
        hdscling = false;
    },100);
})
</script>

<script>
$(function(){
    autoLogin();
});

function autoLogin(){
    var memindex = "";
    var autoLogin = "";

    if( memindex == '' && autoLogin == '1' ){
        $.ajax({
            url:'/member/auto_loginpro.php',
            type:'post',
            async:false,
            success:function(data){
                console.log(data);
                if( data ){
                    alert(data);
                }else{
                    location.replace('/');
                }
            }
        });
    }
}
$(window).scroll(function(){
	var scl = $(document).scrollTop();
	if(scl > 100){
		$(".quick_delivery , .btn_quick, #today_timediv").css({'opacity':'1','z-index':'10001'});
	}else if( !$('.rightPanelClose').hasClass('on') ){
		$(".quick_delivery , .btn_quick, #today_timediv").css({'opacity':'0','z-index':'20'});
	}
});
$("a[data-clickMap='main-장바구니버튼']").closest('.fixno').find('.cnt').text('');

$(document).on('mouseover',".colorCode",function(){
    var obj = $(event.target||event.srcElement);
    var li = obj.closest('li');
    var img = li.find(".prdimg a img");
    var op1name = li.find(".op1name");
    var orisrc = li.find(".prdimg a img").data("oriimg");
    if(orisrc && obj.data("colorsrc")){
        // op1name.text(obj.data("colorname"))
        img.attr("src",obj.data("colorsrc"));
    }

})
$(document).on('mouseleave',".colorCodebox",function(){
    var obj = $(event.target||event.srcElement);
    var li = obj.closest('li');
    var img = li.find(".prdimg a img");
    var op1name = li.find(".op1name");

    op1name.text('')
    var orisrc = li.find(".prdimg a img").data("oriimg");
    img.attr("src",orisrc);
})
function delrecent(e){
	if(!confirm("검색어 "+e+"를 삭제 하시겠습니까?"))return;
	var obj = $(event.target||event.srcElement);
	$.ajax({
        url:'/ajax_proc/etc.php',
		type:'post',
		async:false,
		data:{"mode":"delrecent","delkeyword":e},
		success:function(code){
			if(code) eval(code);
			obj.closest("li").remove();
		}
	});
}
</script>

<script>
    // var wish_list = new Swiper('.wish-list-folders', {
    //     slidesPerView: 4.3,
    //     spaceBetween: 7,
    //     observer: true,
    //     observeParents: true,
    // });


    function wish_folder() {


        this.init = function() {
            this.selectedImg = ''; //찜할당시 찜한상품의 이미지 상품정보 idx,이미지경로
            this.goods_idx = []; //찜할당시 선택한 상품
            this.selectFolder = ''; //현재 선택된 폴더
            this.firstpopup = $('.wish-select-folder-popup'); //첫 녹색팝업
            this.wishfolderWrap = $('.wish-folder-wrap'); //폴더추가 전체랩
            this.wishlistWrap = $('.wish-list-wrap'); //폴더리스트,찜리스트 랩
            this.wishsettingWrap = $('.wish-setting-wrap'); //폴더편집 랩
            this.wishaddfolder = $('.wish-add-folder'); //폴더추가 팝업
            this.selectli = $('#OPEN-FOLDER-HTML').html(); //폴더선택 li html
            this.slideli = $('#FOLDER-SWIPER-LI').html(); //찜한 상품 폴더목록 슬라이드 html
            this.wishListli = $('#WISH-LIST-LI').html(); //찜한상품 상품목록 html
            this.settingfolderlist = $('#WISH-LIST-SETTING-LI').html(); //폴더편집내 폴더목록 html
            this.selectFolder_complete = $('.wish-select-folder-popup-select');
            this.scrollTop = 0;
            this.wishAddCartWrap = $('#wish-add-cart-wrap'); //장바구니담기 랩
            this.wishAddCarthtml = $('#WISH_CART').html(); //장바구니 상품 html
            this.sort = sessionStorage.getItem('orby') ? sessionStorage.getItem('orby') : 99; //리스트 정렬기준
            this.sdata = ""; // 상품시즌

            var obj = this;
            var firstpopup = obj.firstpopup;
            var wishfolderWrap = obj.wishfolderWrap;
            var wishlistWrap = obj.wishlistWrap;
            var wishsettingWrap = obj.wishsettingWrap;
            var wishaddfolder = obj.wishaddfolder;
            $(function(){
                if(wishlistWrap.css("display") == "block"){
                    // $('.fixed-bar').show();
                }
            })
            //좋아요시 팝업출력
            $(document).on('click', '.wishcart span.wish', function() {
                if ($(this).hasClass('on') && firstpopup.css('display') == 'none' ) {
                    firstpopup.fadeIn();
                    obj.selectedImg = $(this).closest('.goods-form-list').find('.prdimg img').attr('src');
                    obj.goods_idx.push($(this).attr('onclick').match(/^.*\(\'?\"?(.*)\'?\"?\).*?;/)[1]);
                    setTimeout(function() {
                        firstpopup.fadeOut();
                    }, 3500);
                }

            })

            //미선택 테두리제거
            $(document).on('change', 'select.unselected', function() {
                $(this).removeClass('unselected');
            })

            //장바구니닫기
            $(document).on('click','#wish-add-cart-wrap',function(e){
                var t = $(e.target).attr('id');
                if(t == 'wish-add-cart-wrap'){
                    wish_folder.wishListAddCartClose();
                }
            });





            //폴더창 외부클릭 폴더창 닫기
            wishfolderWrap.click(function(e) {
                var outcheck = $(e.target).hasClass('wish-folder-wrap');
                if (outcheck) {
                    obj.closewindow();
                    obj.wishListModdify();
                    obj.folderAddCancel();
                }

            })


            wishaddfolder.find('input[name=folder_name]').on('input propertychange', function() {
                if (this.value.length > 0) {
                    wishaddfolder.find('.okbtn').addClass('on');
                } else {
                    wishaddfolder.find('.okbtn').removeClass('on');
                }
            })


            //폴더설정 닫기
            wishsettingWrap.click(function() {




                var obj = $(event.target);
                if(obj.closest(".wish-setting-content").length < 1 || obj == $(".setting-clsbtn")) {
                    wish_folder.folderAddCancel()
                    wishsettingWrap.hide();
                }
                return false;
            });



        }


        this.selectAllWishList = function() {
            var t = $(event.target).closest('.selectall');
            var state = t.find('.wish-select-all');
            var wl = this.wishlistWrap.find('.wish-list-goodslist-ul li');
            state.toggleClass('on');
            if (state.hasClass('on')) {
                wl.find(".prdcheckbox").addClass('wish_checked');
            } else {
                wl.find(".prdcheckbox").removeClass('wish_checked');
                $('.goods-form-list .opt_layer').each(function(){
                    if( $(this).css('display') == 'none' ){
                        return true;
                    }
                    $(this).closest('.goods-form-list').find('.wish-cart-open').click();

                })
            }

            var len = document.querySelectorAll('.wish_checked').length;
            document.querySelector('#wishChkCnt').innerText = len;
        }

        this.sdataChange = function(){
            var t = $(event.target);
            this.sdata = t.val();
            this.WishlistFolderSelect();
        }

        this.sortChange = function() {
            var t = $(event.target);
            this.sort = t.val();
            this.WishlistFolderSelect();
            sessionStorage.setItem('orby',t.val());
        }

        this.wishMoveCart = function() {

            //모바일
            // var rt = GoodsProc.codi_set_cart();
            // rt.then((value) => {
            //     if (value === undefined) {
            //         this.wishListAddCartClose();
            //         this.wishListModdify()
            //     } else {
            //         //실패시 선택안한옵션 테두리적용
            //         this.wishAddCartWrap.find('select[name^=op]').each(function() {
            //             if ($(this).val() == '') {
            //                 $(this).addClass('unselected');
            //             }
            //         });
            //     }
            // });

            //피씨
            var status = true;
            var objarr = [];
            if( $('.wish_checked').length == 0 ){
                alert('상품을 선택해주세요');
                return;
            }

            $('.wish_checked').each(function(){
                var obj = $(this).closest('.goods-form-list').find('.opt_layer');
                if(obj.css('display') == 'none'){
                    obj.closest('.box').find('.wish-cart-open').click();
                }
                var op1 = obj.find('select[name=op1] option:selected');
                var op2 = obj.find('select[name=op2] option:selected');

                if( !op1.val() ){
                    objarr.push(op1);
                    status = false;
                }
                if( !op2.val() ){
                    objarr.push(op2);
                    status = false;
                }
            })

            if(!status){
                for( var i in objarr){
                    objarr[i].parent().addClass('unselected');
                }
                var sc = $('.unselected').closest('.opt_layer').offset().top;

                if(sc){
                    $(window).scrollTop(sc-600);
                }
                alert('상품을 선택해주세요');
                $(".unselected").closest('.goods-form-list').find('.opt_layer').show();
                return false;
            }

            var string = [];
            $('.wish_checked').each(function(){
                var tmp = [];
                var t = $(this).closest('.goods-form-list');
                var goods_idx = $(this).data('idx');
                var op1 = t.find('[name="op1"]').val();
                var op2 = t.find('[name="op2"]').val();
                var ea = t.find('[name="ea"]').val();

                if( !ea ){
                    ea = 1;
                }

                if( !goods_idx ){
                    return true;
                }

                tmp.push( goods_idx );

                if( op1 ){
                    tmp.push(op1);
                }

                if( op2 ){
                    tmp.push(op2);
                }

                tmp = tmp.join('_')+'/'+ea;
                string.push(tmp);
                t.find('.wish-cart-open').click();
            });

            GoodsProc.ajax('set_cart',string);
            if( confirm("장바구니에 담았습니다.\n\n장바구니로 이동하시겠습니까?")){
                location.href = '/member/cart.php';
            }

        }
        this.wishListAddCart = function() {
            var data = {
                'method': 'getGoodsOption',
                'goods_idx': []
            };
            // this.wishlistWrap.find('.wish_checked').each(function() {
            $('.wish_checked').each(function() {
                if($(this).data('idx')){
                    data.goods_idx.push($(this).data('idx'));
                }
            });
            if (data.goods_idx.length == 0) {
                alert('상품을 선택해주세요');
                return;
            }
            var returndata = JSON.parse(this.ajax(data));
            if (returndata.length == 0) {
                alert('비정상 접근입니다');
                return;
            }
            this.scrollTop = $(window).scrollTop();
            $('html,body').addClass('scrollOff');
            this.wishAddCartWrap.show();
            var cartwrap = this.wishAddCartWrap.find('.cart-goods-list-wrap');
            var carthtml = this.wishAddCarthtml;
            $.each(returndata, function(i, v) {

                cartwrap.append(carthtml);
                var added = cartwrap.find('.cart-goods-list').last();
                added.find('.img img').attr('src', v.img);
                added.find('.gname').text(v.gname);
                added.find('.price .num').text(v.faccount);
                added.find('.price percent').remove().append(v.discountPer);
                added.find('input[name=goods_idx]').val(v.index_no);
                added.find('select').remove();
                added.addClass('goods-codi-form');
                if (v.useop1 == 'Y') {
                    var op1 = '<select name="op1" onchange="GoodsProc.codi_set_opt();" data-goods_idx="' + (v.index_no) + '">';
                    op1 += '<option value="">옵션 1 ) 선택해주세요</option>';

                    $.each(v.op1, function(o1, o1v) {
                        op1 += '<option value="' + (o1v.idx) + '" data-opname="' + (o1v.name) + '" data-account="' + (o1v.account) + '" data-ispluslimit="' + (o1v.isplus_limit) + '">' + (o1v.name) + '</option>';
                    });
                    op1 += '</select>';
                    added.find('.pop1').append(op1);
                }
                if (v.useop2 == 'Y') {
                    var op2 = '<select name="op2" class="op-select" onchange="GoodsProc.codi_set_opt();" >';
                    op2 += '<option value="">색상 선택 후 선택</option>';
                    op2 += '</select>';
                    added.find('.pop2').append(op2);
                }

            })

        }
        this.wishListAddCartClose = function() {
            $('html,body').removeClass('scrollOff');
            $(window).scrollTop(this.scrollTop);
            this.wishAddCartWrap.hide();
            this.wishAddCartWrap.find('.cart-goods-list-wrap').empty();
        }
        this.updateFolderName = function() {
            var t = $(event.target);
            if (t.val().length == 0) {
                alert('폴더명을 입력해주세요');
                return false;
            }
            var idx = t.closest('li.wish-folders-item').data('idx');
            var data = {
                'method': 'updateFolderName',
                'index_no': idx,
                'name': t.val()
            }
            var returndata = this.ajax(data);
        }
        this.wishListMove = function() {
            //선택한상품 폴더변경
            this.goods_idx = [];
            var obj = this;
            this.wishlistWrap.find('.wish_checked').each(function() {
                obj.goods_idx.push($(this).data('idx'));
            });
            if (this.goods_idx.length == 0) {
                alert('상품을 선택해주세요');
                return;
            } else {
                this.showFolderList();
                this.wishListModdify(1);

            }


        }
        this.wishListAction = function() {
            //선택한상품 삭제,상단이동
            if (!event) {
                return false;
            }
            var t = $(event.target);
            var method = t.attr('class');

            var data = {
                'method': method,
                'goods_idx': [],
            }

            this.wishlistWrap.find('.wish_checked').each(function() {
                data.goods_idx.push($(this).data('idx'));
            })

            if (data.goods_idx.length == 0) {
                alert('상품을 선택해주세요');
                return;
            } else {
                var returndata = this.ajax(data);
                this.WishListadd(this.selectFolder);
                this.wishListModdify(1);
            }
        }

        this.wishListcheck = function() {
            //찜상품 삭제,이동,정렬 상품클릭
            var t = $(event.target).closest('.prdcheckbox');
            t.toggleClass('wish_checked');
            if(!t.hasClass('wish_checked')){
                this.wishlistWrap.find('.selectall .wish-select-all').removeClass('on');
            }
            if( t.closest('.goods-form-list').find('.opt_layer').css('display') != 'none' && !t.hasClass('wish_checked')){
                t.closest('.goods-form-list').find('.wish-cart-open').click();
            }

            var len = document.querySelectorAll('.wish_checked').length;
            document.querySelector('#wishChkCnt').innerText = len;
        }
        this.wishListModdify = function(close) {

            //찜상품 삭제,이동,정렬변경 버튼 팝업열기

            //     this.wishlistWrap.find('.selectall').hide().find('.wish-select-all').removeClass('on');

        this.closefolderSetting = function() {
            // this.folderListaddli();
            this.wishsettingWrap.hide();

        }

        this.closewindow = function() {
            $('html,body').removeClass('scrollOff');
            $(window).scrollTop(this.scrollTop);

            this.wishfolderWrap.hide();
        }

        this.folderSetting = function() {
            //찜한상품내 폴더세팅버튼
            this.wishsettingWrap.show();
            //WISH-LIST-SETTING-LI
            var data = {
                'method': 'folderSetting'
            }
            var ul = this.wishsettingWrap.find('.wish-folders-wrap ul');
            var li = this.settingfolderlist;
            var returndata = JSON.parse(this.ajax(data));
            ul.empty().append(li);
            ul.find('li .delbtn').text('');
            ul.find('li .item-box').val('기본 폴더').prop('readonly', true);
            ul.find('li .movebtn').text('');
            $.each(returndata, function(i, v) {
                ul.append(li);
                var added = ul.children().last();
                added.find('.item-box').val(v.name);
                added.data('idx', v.index_no);

            })
            var obj = this;
            ul.sortable({
                handle: ".movebtn",
                axis: "y",
                items: "li:gt(0)",
                start: function(evt, ui) {},
                stop: function(evt, ui) {
                    // console.log(ui.item.data('idx'));
                    //폴더정렬업데이트
                    obj.folderSetsort();
                }
            });
        }
        this.folderSetsort = function() {
            //드래그드롭으로 폴더정렬
            var ul = this.wishsettingWrap.find('.wish-folders-wrap ul');
            var data = {
                'method': 'folderSetsort',
                'sortdata': []
            };
            $.each(ul.children('li'), function(i, v) {
                var folder_idx = $(v).data('idx');
                if (folder_idx) {
                    data.sortdata.push(folder_idx);
                }
            })
            var returndata = this.ajax(data);

            //정렬후 리스트초기화
            this.folderListaddli()
        }
        this.folderAdd = function() {
            //폴더선택 상단 폴더추가
            $('.wish-setting-content').hide();
            $('.wish-select-folder').hide();
            if (this.selectedImg) {
                this.wishaddfolder.find('.folder-img').attr('src', this.selectedImg);
            } else {
                this.wishaddfolder.find('.folder-img').attr('src', 'https://atimg.sonyunara.com/2021/renewal/wish_common/cartdefault2.png?v=202605201633');
            }
            this.wishaddfolder.show();


            this.wishsettingWrap.addClass('on');
            this.wishfolderWrap.find('.wish-select-folder').addClass('on');
        }
        this.ajax = function(dataobj) {
            var returnData = '';
            $.ajax({
                url: '/_wish_folder.php',
                data: dataobj,
                type: 'post',
                async: false,
                success: function(v) {
                    returnData = v;
                }
            })
            return returnData;
        }
        this.showDeliveryType = function(type) {
            var t = $(event.target).closest('.' + type);

            t.toggleClass('on');
            t.find('img').toggle();
            var nowstat = t.hasClass('on');
            var typelist = [];
            t.closest('.delivery-btnul-wish').find('li.on').each(function() {
                typelist.push($(this).data('type'));
            })
            this.wishlistWrap.find('.wish-list-goodslist-ul > li').show();
            for (i in typelist) {

                this.wishlistWrap.find('.wish-list-goodslist-ul > li').each(function() {
                    if ($(this).data(typelist[i]) != 'Y') {
                        $(this).hide();
                    }
                })
            }

            document.querySelectorAll('.wish_checked').forEach(e=>{
                e.classList.remove('wish_checked');
            })
            var len = document.querySelectorAll('.wish_checked').length;
            document.querySelector('#wishChkCnt').innerText = len;
            return;

        }
        this.folderListaddli = function() {
            //폴더목록 비동기 li넣어주는함수

            var data = {
                'method': 'folderSetting'
            }
            var returndata = JSON.parse(this.ajax(data));
            var update_section = {
                0: { //찜한목록 폴더리스트
                    ul: this.wishlistWrap.find('.wish-list-folders > ul'),
                    li: this.slideli
                },
                1: { //선택폴더 폴더리스트
                    ul: this.wishfolderWrap.find('.wish-folder-list'),
                    li: this.selectli
                }
            };

            //폴더리스트 업데이트
            $('.folderul').slick('slickRemove',null,null,true);
            $.each(update_section, function(num, parent) {

                var ul = parent.ul;
                var li = parent.li;

                // ul.empty().append(li);
                if(num == 0){
                    ul.slick('slickAdd',li); //찜한목록 폴더리스트만 슬릭
                }else{
                    ul.empty().append(li);
                }
                $.each(returndata, function(i, v) {
                    if(num == 0){
                        ul.slick('slickAdd',li);
                    }else{
                        ul.append(li);
                    }

                    var added = ul.find('li').last();
                    added.data('idx', v.index_no).find('p.name').text(v.name);

                    if (v.img) {
                        added.find('.img img').attr('src', v.img);
                    }
                });
                if(num == 0){
                    ul.slick('slickAdd',li); //찜한목록 폴더리스트만 슬릭
                    var added = ul.find('li').last();
                    added.data('idx', 'folder-setting').find('p.name').text('폴더수정');
                    added.attr('onclick','wish_folder.folderSetting()');
                    added.find('.img img').attr('src','https://atimg.sonyunara.com/2021/renewal/wish_common/foldersettingbtn.png').css('border-radius','0');
                    $('.folderul').slick('setPosition');
                }else{
                    ul.append(li);
                    var added = ul.find('li').last();
                    added.data('idx', 'folder-add').find('p.name').text('폴더추가');
                    added.find('.img img').attr('src','https://atimg.sonyunara.com/2021/renewal/wish_common/folderaddbtn.png');
                    added.attr('onclick','wish_folder.folderAdd()');
                }

            })

            //슬라이드 업데이트
            // wish_list.update();
            //업데이트된폴더 on 클래스추가
            this.folderSelectaddClass(this.selectFolder);


        }
        this.folderSelectaddClass = function(idx) {
            //선택된폴더 on 클래스
            var selectedli = this.wishlistWrap.find('.wish-list-folders > ul').find('li');
            selectedli.removeClass('on');
            $.each(selectedli, function(i, v) {
                if ($(this).data('idx') == idx) {
                    $(this).addClass('on');
                    // wish_list.slideTo(i);
                }
            })

        }
        this.WishListadd = function(idx) {
            //찜한상품 목록내 상품목록 비동기 li추가
            this.wishlistWrap.find(".thico").removeClass('on');
            var data = {
                'method': 'WishListadd',
                'folder_idx': idx,
                'sdata':this.sdata,
                'orby': this.sort
            }

            var returndata = this.ajax(data);
            var ul = this.wishlistWrap.find('.wish-list-goodslist-ul');
            ul.empty();
            ul.append(returndata);

            this.folderSelectaddClass(idx);


        }
        this.folderSelect = function() {
            //찜한상품 팝업(폴더목록 in 상품목록)
            var t = $(event.target).closest('.open-folder');
            var folder_idx = t.data('idx');
            this.selectFolder = folder_idx;
            if (this.goods_idx.length > 0) {
                //찜한다음 폴더선택시 해당폴더로 업데이트
                this.folderIdx_update(folder_idx, this.goods_idx);
            }
            this.folderListaddli();
            this.closewindow(); //폴더세팅보여주기에서 창닫기로변경
            this.WishlistFolderSelect();
            return;
            this.WishListadd(folder_idx);

            this.wishlistWrap.show();

        }
        this.folderIdx_update = function(folder_idx, goods_idx) {
            //찜한상품 선택한 폴더 밑으로 수정 goods_idx 배열로받음
            if( goods_idx.length == 0 ){
                return;
            }
            var data = {
                'method': 'folderIdx_update',
                'folder_idx': folder_idx,
                'goods_idx': goods_idx
            }
            var update = this.ajax(data);
            this.goods_idx = []; //찜한상품 폴더지정했으니 초기화
            this.selectedImg = ''; //폴더이미지 업로드했으니 초기화
            var complete_html = this.selectFolder_complete;
            complete_html.find('span').text(update);
            complete_html.fadeIn();
            var obj  = this;
            setTimeout(function(){
                complete_html.fadeOut();
            },2000)
        }
        this.showFolderList = function() {
            this.scrollTop = $(window).scrollTop();
            $('html,body').addClass('scrollOff');

            //팝업 폴더선택하기 클릭 폴더리스트 표시
            this.wishfolderWrap.show();
            this.firstpopup.hide();

            this.folderListaddli();
        }

        this.WishlistFolderSelect = function() {
            //찜한상품 목록내 폴더선택
            var t = $(event.target).closest('.folderli');
            if (t[0]) {
                this.selectFolder = t.data('idx');
            }
            this.WishListadd(this.selectFolder);
            this.wishListModdify(1);

            //오늘출발 무료배송 초기화
            this.wishlistWrap.find('.delivery-type-btn li').each(function() {
                $(this).removeClass('on');
                $(this).find('img').eq(0).show();
                $(this).find('img').eq(1).hide();
            })
        }

        this.delFolder = function() {
            if (!confirm('폴더를 삭제하시겠습니까?')) {
                return false;
            }
            var t = $(event.target).closest('.wish-folders-item');
            var data = {
                'method': 'delFolder',
                'folder_idx': t.data('idx'),
            }
            var returndata = this.ajax(data);
            t.remove();

            this.folderListaddli();

        }
        this.addFolder = function() {

            //폴더리스트->폴더추가
            var t = $(event.target);
            if (!t.hasClass('on')) {
                alert('폴더명을 입력해주세요');
                t.focus();
                return false;
            }
            var input = this.wishaddfolder.find('input[name=folder_name]');

            var data = {
                'method': 'addFolder',
                'name': input.val(),
                'img': this.selectedImg,
            };

            var folder_idx = Number(this.ajax(data));
            if (folder_idx) {
                alert('폴더가 추가됐습니다');
                $('.wish-setting-content').show();
                input.val('');
                this.folderIdx_update(folder_idx, this.goods_idx);
                this.folderAddCancel(); //창닫기

                if (this.wishlistWrap.find('.wish-setting-wrap').css('display') == 'block') {
                    //폴더세팅 목록업데이트
                    this.folderSetting();
                }
                //폴더목록 업데이트
                this.folderListaddli();

                return;
            } else {
                alert('이미 존재하는 이름입니다');
                return;
            }
        }
        this.folderAddCancel = function() {
            //폴더추가 취소버튼
            this.wishaddfolder.hide();
            this.wishfolderWrap.find('.wish-select-folder').removeClass('on');
            this.wishsettingWrap.removeClass('on');
            $('.wish-setting-content').show();
            $('.wish-select-folder').show();
        }

        this.closeWishlist = function() {
            //찜한상품 목록 닫기
            //this.wishlistWrap.hide();
            history.back();
        }

        this.toggleCart = function(t){
            var t = $(t);
            t.parents('.box').find('.opt_layer').slideToggle(); t.toggleClass('on');
            if(t.hasClass('on')){
                t.text('옵션 닫기');
            }else{
                t.text('옵션 선택');
            }
        }


    }
    var wish_folder = new wish_folder();
    wish_folder.init();

<script>
    function Lab() {
        this.setWrap = $('.lab-set-pick-wrap');
        this.guideWrap = $('.guide-pop-wrap');
        this.menuhtml = $('#LAB-MENU-LIST').html();
        this.pickmenuhtml = $('#LAB-PICK-MENU').html();
        this.firstLoading = true;
        this.currentType;
        this.scrollTop = 0;
        this.init = function() {

            var obj = this;
            $('.bgwrap').click(function(e) {
                if ($(e.target).hasClass('bgwrap')) {
                    obj.closePop();
                }
            })
            // $(document).on('keyup', '.set-category-search input[name=searchPick]', function(e) {
            //     if (e.keyCode == 13) {
            //         $(this).blur();
            //     }

            //     obj.searchPick($(this).val());
            // })
            //this.showPop();
        }
        this.showPop = function() {
            if (this.firstLoading) {
                this.tabChange('category');
            }

            this.getUserData();

            //$("html , body").css({
            //    //'overflow-y': 'hidden',
            //    'height':'100%'
            //});

            this.scrollTop = $(window).scrollTop();
            // $('html,body').addClass('scrollOff');

            $('#mypick-menu').hide();
        }
        this.closeWindow = function() {
            if( window.location.href.indexOf('/member/lab.php') > 0 ){
                history.back();
                return;
            }
            $('#att-lab').hide();
            //$("html , body").css({
            //    'overflow-y': 'auto',
            //});
            $('html,body').removeClass('scrollOff');
            $(window).scrollTop(this.scrollTop);
        }
        this.closePop = function() {
            // this.setWrap.hide();
            // this.guideWrap.hide();

            //$('#att-lab').css({
            //    'overflow-y': 'auto',
            //});
            $('body,html').removeClass('scrollOff');
            $('#att-lab').hide();
        }
        this.pickMenuShow = function() {
            if( $('.btn_qucik_wrap > .rightPanelClose').hasClass('on') ){
                console.log(123)
                $('.btn_qucik_wrap > .rightPanelClose').click();
            }
            var wrap = $('#mypick-menu');
            var lihtml = $('#LAB-PICKED-LIST').html();

            var data = {
                'method': 'getUserData',
                'orby': 'desc'
            }
            var returndata = JSON.parse(this.ajax(data));
            var json = returndata.pickmenu;
            wrap.find('ul.mypick-menu-icon li.picked').remove();
            wrap.find('ul.mypick-menu-icon').prepend(lihtml);
            wrap.find('ul.mypick-menu-icon li').addClass('addBtn');
            wrap.find('ul.mypick-menu-icon li.picked a').attr('href', 'javascript:attLab.showPop();attLab.addPickMenu();');
            if (json) {
                for (i in json) {
                    var j = json[i];
                    wrap.find('ul.mypick-menu-icon').prepend(lihtml);
                    var added = wrap.find('ul.mypick-menu-icon li.picked').first();
                    added.find('img').attr('src', j.imgsrc+"?v=12");
                    added.find('a').attr('href', j.href);
                    added.find('.picked-text a').text(j.name);
                }
                if(json.length >= 5){
                    var modify =  wrap.find('ul.mypick-menu-icon li.picked').last();
                    modify.find('.picked-text a').text('수정');
                    modify.find('img').attr('src',  modify.find('img').attr('src').replace('add.png','edit.png') );
                }
            }

            wrap.toggle();
        }
        // this.searchPick = function(v) {
        //     if (v == '') {
        //         $('.set-menu-list li').show();
        //         return;
        //     }
        //     $('.set-menu-list li').each(function() {
        //         if ($(this).text().indexOf(v) > 0) {
        //             $(this).show();
        //         } else {
        //             $(this).hide();
        //         }
        //     })
        // }
        // this.searchOpen = function() {
        //     this.currentType = $('.set-category li.on').data('type');

        //     this.tabChange('category');
        //     this.tabChange('brand');
        //     this.tabChange('seleb');
        //     this.tabChange('mypage');
        //     this.tabChange('event');
        //     this.tabChange('theme');
        //     $('.set-menu-list').children('div').show();
        //     $('.set-category-search').css('display', 'flex');
        //     $('.set-category-search input[name=searchPick]').focus();
        // }
        // this.searchClose = function() {

        //     $('.set-category-search input[name=searchPick]').val('');
        //     $('.set-menu-list div[class^=menu-] li').show();
        //     this.tabChange(this.currentType);
        //     $('.set-category-search').css('display', 'none');
        // }
        // this.guideOpen = function() {
        //     this.guideWrap.css({
        //         'display': 'flex'
        //     });

        this.removePickMenu = function() {
            // $('ul.picks').height($('ul.picks').outerHeight());
            var t = $(event.target).closest('li');
            var data = {
                'method': 'menuSelect',
                'type': t.data('type'),
                'type_idx': t.data('type_idx')
            }
            var returndata = this.ajax(data);
            if (returndata == 'delete') {
                this.firstLoading = true;
                this.getUserData();

                $('div.menu-' + data.type).find('li').each(function() {
                    if ($(this).data('type_idx') == '_' + data.type_idx) {
                        $(this).removeClass('on');//find('.list-menu-btn img').attr('src', $(this).find('.list-menu-btn img').attr('src').replace('on.png', 'off.png'))
                    }
                })
                //


            }

        }
        this.addPickMenu = function() {
            $("#att-lab").show();
            // this.setWrap.show();
            // $('#att-lab').css({
            //    'overflow-y': 'hidden',
            // });
            $('body,html').addClass('scrollOff');
        }
        this.getUserData = function() {

            if (!this.firstLoading) {
                return false;
            }

            var data = {
                'method': 'getUserData'
            }
            var returndata = this.ajax(data);
            var json = JSON.parse(returndata);
            var col = $('col-select > btwrap');
            if ($(".btimgwrap[data-type='" + json.col + "']")) {
                $(".btimgwrap[data-type='" + json.col + "']").addClass('on');
            }

            //움직이는 이미지 멈추기 버튼
            // var img = $('.git-stop-btn img');
            // json.gifstop = json.gifstop ?  json.gifstop : 'off';
            // img.attr('src', img.attr('src').replace(/gif_[a-z]{2,3}.png/,"/gif_"+json.gifstop+".png")  );


            var html = this.pickmenuhtml;
            $('ul.picks').each(function(uli) {

                $(this).empty();
                for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
                    var j;
                    if (json.pickmenu) {
                        j = json.pickmenu[i];
                    }
                    $(this).append(html);
                    var li = $(this).find('li').eq(i);
                    if (j) {
                        var imgtmp = new Image();
                        imgtmp.src = j.imgsrc;
                        li.find('.list-icon img').attr('src', j.imgsrc+'?v=12');
                        li.find('.list-content').text(j.name);
                        li.find('.remove-pick').show();
                        li.data('type', j.type).data('type_idx', j.type_idx);
                        if( $(this).hasClass('pop-picks') ){
                            li.find('.list-icon').attr('onclick','location.href="'+j.href+'"');
                            li.find('.list-content').attr('onclick','location.href="'+j.href+'"');
                        }

                    } else if (uli > 0) {
                        // li.find('.list-content').text('추가');
                    }


                }
            });


            this.firstLoading = false;

        }
        // this.colChange = function() {
        //     var t = $(event.target).closest('div.btimgwrap');
        //     var col = t.data('type');
        //     var data = {
        //         'method': 'colChange',
        //         'type': col
        //     }
        //     var returndata = this.ajax(data);
        //     if (returndata != 'N') {
        //         $('div.btimgwrap').removeClass('on');
        //         t.addClass('on');
        //         $('.goods-list ul.col2, .goods-list ul.col3 , ul.col2.scrollList, ul.col3.scrollList').each(function() {
        //             $(this).removeClass('col2').removeClass('col3').addClass('col' + col);
        //         });

        //     } else {
        //         alert('로그인후 사용 가능합니다');
        //         return false;
        //     }
        // }
        this.menuSelect = function() {
            var t = $(event.target).closest('li');
            // var current = t.find('.list-menu-btn img').attr('src');


            var data = {
                'method': 'menuSelect',
                'name': t.data('name'),
                'href': t.data('href'),
                'imgsrc': t.data('imgsrc'),
                'type': t.data('type'),
                'type_idx': t.data('type_idx')
            }
            var returndata = this.ajax(data);
            if (returndata == 'N') {
                alert('로그인후 사용 가능합니다');
                return;
            }
            if (returndata == 'insert') {
                t.addClass("on");
                // current = current.replace('off.png', 'on.png');
            } else if (returndata == 'delete') {
                t.removeClass("on");

                // current = current.replace('on.png', 'off.png');
            } else {
                alert('Pick메뉴는 최대 5개까지 설정 가능합니다');
                return;
            }
            // t.find('.list-menu-btn img').attr('src', current);

            this.firstLoading = true;
            this.getUserData();


        }
        this.tabChange = function(type) {

            if (event) {
                if ($(event.target).hasClass('att-tab')) {
                    $(event.target).addClass('on').siblings().removeClass('on');
                }

            }

            var tab = $('.set-menu-list');
            tab.scrollTop(0);
            tab.children().hide();
            var currentTab = tab.find('.menu-' + type);
            currentTab.show();
            if (currentTab.find('li').length > 0) {
                return false;
            }
            var data = {
                'method': 'tabChange',
                'type': type
            };
            var returndata = this.ajax(data);
            if (returndata) {
                var j = JSON.parse(returndata);
                for (k in j) {
                    var son = j[k];
                    currentTab.children('ul').append(this.menuhtml)
                    var added = currentTab.find('li').last();
                    added.find('.list-menu-icon img').attr('src', son.imgurl+"?v=12");
                    added.find('.list-menu-title').text(son.name);
                    added.data('imgsrc', son.imgurl).data('href', son.linkurl).data('type', type).data('type_idx', k).data('name', son.name);
                    if (son.check == 'on') {
                        added.addClass('on');
                        // added.find('.list-menu-btn img').attr('src', added.find('.list-menu-btn img').attr('src').replace('off.png', 'on.png'));
                    }

                }
            }

        }
        this.gifStopChange = function(){
            var data = {
                'method': 'gifStopChange'
            };

            var returndata = this.ajax(data);
            if(returndata == 'N'){
                alert('로그인후 사용 가능합니다');
                return false;
            }
            $(".gifonoff").toggleClass('on');

            //움직이는 이미지 멈추기 버튼
            // var img = $('.git-stop-btn img');
            // img.attr('src', img.attr('src').replace(/gif_[a-z]{2,3}.png/,"/gif_"+returndata+".png")  );
            // sessionStorage.setItem('listLoc','');
            if( confirm('새로고침 후에 적용됩니다 새로고침 하시겠습니까?') ){
                location.reload();
            }

        }
        this.ajax = function(dataobj) {
            var returnData = '';
            $.ajax({
                url: '/_lab.php',
                type: 'post',
                data: dataobj,
                async: false,
                success: function(v) {

                    returnData = v;

                }
            })
            return returnData;
        }

    }
    var attLab = new Lab();
    attLab.init();
    // attLab.showPop();

                <script>
                    document.addEventListener('click',function(){

                        if( !event.target.closest('.header-burger') && !event.target.closest('.header-menu') ){
                            document.querySelector('.header-burger').style.display = 'none';
                        }

                        ele.addEventListener('focus',function(){
                            // this.style.width = '130px';
                        })
                        ele.addEventListener('blur',function(){
                            // this.style.width = '70px';
                        })
                    })
                    function openHeaderSearch(){
                        document.querySelector('.header-burger').style.display = 'none';
                        document.querySelector('.header-search').style.display='block';
                    }

<script type='text/javascript'>
  //<![CDATA[
    // 사용할 앱의 JavaScript 키를 설정해 주세요.
    Kakao.init('b14f716a6930574535cecb5ab4c88c68');
    // 카카오 로그인 버튼을 생성합니다.
    Kakao.Auth.createLoginButton({
      container: '#kakao-login-btn',
      success: function(authObj) {
        //alert(JSON.stringify(authObj));
        // 로그인 성공시, API를 호출합니다.
        Kakao.API.request({
          url: '/v2/user/me',
          success: function(res) {
            //alert(JSON.stringify(res));
			login_proc(res);
          },
          fail: function(error) {
            alert(JSON.stringify(error));
          }
        });
      },
      fail: function(err) {
		 if( err['error_description'] == 'User denied access' ){
			//alert('취소되었습니다.');
		 }else{
			alert(JSON.stringify(error));
		 }
      }
    });
  //]]>
 function kakao_login_call(){
	
	Kakao.Auth.login({
		success: function(authObj) {

			// 로그인 성공시, API를 호출합니다.
			Kakao.API.request({
				url: '/v2/user/me',
				success: function(res) {
					login_proc(res);
					
				},
				fail: function(err) {
					 if( err['error_description'] == 'User denied access' ){
						//alert('취소되었습니다.');
					 }else{
						alert(JSON.stringify(error));
					 }
				}
			});
		},
		fail: function(err) {
			alert(JSON.stringify(err));
		}
	});
 }

 function kakao_login_call2(){
	Kakao.Auth.login({
		success: function(authObj) {
			// 로그인 성공시, API를 호출합니다.
			Kakao.API.request({
				url: '/v2/user/me',
				success: function(res) {
					//alert(res.id);
					//login_proc(res);

					var id = user_sync_memid(res.id);
					if( id == '' ){
						login_proc(res);
					}else{
						alert(id+" 아이��로 이미 가입되어 있습니다.\n중복 가입은 불가능 합니다.");
						kakao_pop_close();
					}
				},
				fail: function(err) {
					 if( err['error_description'] == 'User denied access' ){
						//alert('취소되었습니다.');
					 }else{
						alert(JSON.stringify(error));
					 }
				}
			});
		},
		fail: function(err) {
			alert(JSON.stringify(err));
		}
	});
 }

 function user_sync_memid(id){
	 var id = $.ajax({
		url:'/api/kakaoSync/memidCheck.php',
		type:'post',
		async:false,
		data:{'id':id},
		success:function(code){
		}
	 }).responseText;

	return id;
 }

 function login_proc(json){
	

	if( json.id == '' ){
		alert("네트워크 에러발생\n문제가 지속될시 고객센터로 문의해주세요.");
		return false;
	}

	$('#kakao_form #id').val(json.id);
	$('#kakao_form #nickname').val(json.properties.nickname);
	$('#kakao_form #cp').val(json.kakao_account.phone_number);
	$('#kakao_form #email').val(json.kakao_account.email);
	$('#kakao_form #birthyear').val(json.kakao_account.birthyear);
	$('#kakao_form #birthday').val(json.kakao_account.birthday);

	$('#kakao_form').submit();
 }
</script>

function google_login_call(login){
		attachSignin(document.getElementById('google_btn'));	
}

function attachSignin(element) {
	auth2.attachClickHandler(element, {},
		function(googleUser) {

			console.log(googleUser.getBasicProfile().getId());

			$('#google_id').val(googleUser.getBasicProfile().getId());
			$('#google_email').val(googleUser.getBasicProfile().getEmail());
			$('#google_name').val(googleUser.getBasicProfile().getName());
			//$('#google_img').val(googleUser.getBasicProfile().getImageUrl());
			window.open('','google_login','width=1024,height=768');
			$('#google_form').submit();
		}, function(error) {
		  //alert(JSON.stringify(error, undefined, 2));
		  //alert("로그인을 취소하였습니다.");
	});
}

</script>

<script>
    document.querySelectorAll('.header-menu > li').forEach(ele=>{
        ele.addEventListener('mouseenter',function(){
            document.querySelectorAll('.header-menu .sub').forEach(ele=> ele.style.display = 'none');
            if( this.querySelector('.sub') ){
                this.querySelector('.sub').style.display = 'block';
            }
        })
        ele.addEventListener('mouseleave',function(){
            document.querySelectorAll('.header-menu .sub').forEach(ele=> ele.style.display = 'none');
        })
    })
</script>

<script>
//무한스크롤
var ajaxPage = 1;
var ajaxLimit = 30;
var loading = false;

$(".goods-cate-btn-copy").append($(".goods-cate-btn:not(.catebtns2) li").clone());

$(window).scroll(function(){
    var st = $(this).scrollTop();

    var ot = $(".goods-cate-btn").offset().top;
    if(st > ot){
        $(".goods-cate-btn-copy").show();
    }else{
        $(".goods-cate-btn-copy").hide();
    }
})
function loadHTML(after){
    if(loading){
        return false;
    }
    loading =  true;
    var cate = $('#cate').val() //== '' ? sessionStorage.getItem('listCate') : $('#cate').val();
    var orby = $('#orby').val() //== '' ? sessionStorage.getItem('listOrby') : $('#orby').val();
    var sdata = $('#sdata').val() //== '' ? sessionStorage.getItem('listOrby') : $('#orby').val();
    var model_idx = $('#model_idx').val();
    var upcate = $('#upcate').val();
    var type = $('#type').val();
    var type2 = $('#type2').val();
    var type3 = $('#type3').val();
    var mcate = $('#mcate').val(); //아뜨제작,단독진행
    var brand = $('#brand').val(); //아뜨랑스,스무레,프롬,아뜨,아띠
    var today = $('.todaybtn').hasClass('on') ? '1' : '0';
    var delFree = $('.freebtn').hasClass('on') ? '1' : '0';
    $.ajax({
                url:'/shop/search.php',
        data:{
            'mode':'loadHTML',
            'page':ajaxPage,
            'limit':ajaxLimit,
            'cate':cate,
            'orby':orby,
            'sdata':sdata,
            'today':today,
            'delFree':delFree,
            'upcate':upcate,
            'model_idx':model_idx,
            'type':type,
            'type2':type2,
            'type3':type3,
            'mcate':mcate,
            'brand':brand,
            'entergoods':'',
            'n':'',
            'index_no':'',
            'head_no':"head_no",
                    },success:function(html){
            if(after == 'Y'){
                $('.scrollList').empty(); // 내용 비우기
            }
            if($.trim(html).length < 3){

                $(window).off('scroll.loadScroll');

                return;
            }
            $('.scrollList').append(html);

            ajaxPage++;
            sessionStorage.setItem('listHtml', $('.scrollList').html());
            sessionStorage.setItem('listPage', Number(ajaxPage));
            sessionStorage.setItem('listLoc', location.href);
            sessionStorage.setItem('listCate', cate);
            sessionStorage.setItem('listOrby', orby);
            sessionStorage.setItem('listSdata', sdata);
            loading = false;

            if(typeof adminOutmallCalc_ === 'function'){
                adminOutmallCalc_(); // _head.php에 있음
            }
            if(typeof skkeyup_ === 'function'){
                skkeyup_(document.querySelector('#search_keywordzz').value); // _head.php에 있음
            }



        }
    })
}
function orbyChange(){
    loading = false;
    var t = $(event.target);
    if(t.val()){
        ajaxPage = 1;
        $('#orby').val(t.val());
        sessionStorage.setItem('listOrby', t.val());

        loadHTML('Y'); // 페이지 가져오기
        scrollInit();
    }
}

function sdataChange(){
    loading = false;
    var t = $(event.target);
    if(t.val()){
        ajaxPage = 1;
        $('#sdata').val(t.val());
        sessionStorage.setItem('listSdata', t.val());

        loadHTML('Y'); // 페이지 가져오기
        scrollInit();
    }
}
function brandChange(){
    loading = false;
    var t = $(event.target);

    ajaxPage = 1;
    $('#brand').val(t.val());
    sessionStorage.setItem('listBrand', t.val());
    loadHTML('Y'); // 페이지 가져오기
    scrollInit();
}
function cateChange(catecode) {
    loading = false;
    ajaxPage = 1;
    // console.log(catecode);
    // var obj = $(event.target);
    $(window).scrollTop($('.goods-cate-btn').offset().top-170);
    if(catecode){
        $('#cate').val(catecode);
    }else if($('#model_idx').val()){
        $('#cate').val('');
    }else if($('#upcate').val()){
        $('#cate').val();
    }else{
        $('#cate').val('');
    }
    sessionStorage.setItem('listCate', catecode);

    $('.goods-cate-btn li').removeClass('on');
    $(".goods-cate-btn-copy li").removeClass('on');
    if(catecode){
        $("li[data-catecode="+catecode+"]").addClass('on');
    }else{
        $("li[data-catecode='']").addClass('on');
        // obj.addClass('on');
    }
    // $('#cate').val(cate); // 새로운 카테고리

    scrollInit(); // 스크롤 이벤트 바인딩
    loadHTML('Y'); // 페이지 가져오기
}


//기록없으면 목록 불러오기
if( location.href == sessionStorage.getItem('listLoc')){
    var html = sessionStorage.getItem('listHtml');
    if(html){
        $('.scrollList').append(html);

        $('#cate').val(sessionStorage.getItem('listCate'));
        $('#orby').val(sessionStorage.getItem('listOrby'));
        $('#brand').val(sessionStorage.getItem('listBrand'));
        $("li[data-catecode='"+sessionStorage.getItem('listCate')+"']").addClass("on").siblings().removeClass("on");
        ajaxPage = sessionStorage.getItem('listPage') - 0;
        scrollInit();
    }else{
        loadHTML();
        scrollInit();
    }
}else{
    $(function(){
                    loadHTML();
            scrollInit();
            })
}

//오늘출발, 무료배송버튼
$('.delivery-btn').each(function(){
    //버튼 유지
    if (location.href == sessionStorage.getItem('listLoc')) {
        var state = sessionStorage.getItem('list'+$(this).data('type'));
        if(state == 'Y'){
            $(this).addClass('on');
        }
    }else{
        sessionStorage.setItem('list'+$(this).data('type'),'');
    }
})
//오늘출발, 무료배송버튼
$('.delivery-btn').click(function() {
    var t = $(this);
    t.toggleClass('on');
    var typelist = [];
    if( t.hasClass('on') ){
        sessionStorage.setItem('list'+$(this).data('type'),'1');
    }else{
        sessionStorage.setItem('list'+$(this).data('type'),'');
    }

    t.closest('ul').find('.delivery-btn').each(function() {
        if($(this).hasClass('on')){
            typelist.push($(this).data('type'));
        }

    })
    $('.scrollList > li').show();
    for (i in typelist) {
        $('.scrollList > li').each(function() {
            if (!$(this).hasClass(typelist[i])) {
                $(this).hide();
            }
        })
    }
    sessionStorage.setItem('listHtml', $('.scrollList').html());

});
function scrollInit() {
    $(window).off('scroll.loadScroll');
    $(window).on('scroll.loadScroll',function(){
        var a = $(this).scrollTop() + screen.availHeight;
        var b = $('body').height();
        if (a / b * 100 >= 70) {
            loadHTML();
        }
    });
}
</script>
	</div>
</div>

<script>
function soldoutCheck(){
	var obj = $(event.target||event.srcElement);

	var soldout = '';
	if( obj.prop('checked') ){
		soldout = 'N';
	}else{
		soldout = '';
	}

	location.href='/shop/search.php?page=1&cate=&soldout='+soldout+'#categoryInfo';
}
</script>

	<script>
	function go_smart_search(){
		var keyword = $('#search_keyword1').val();
		if( !keyword ){
			$('#search_keyword').val('');
		}else{
			$('#search_keyword').val(keyword);
		}

		$('#smartSearchForm').find('input').each(function(){
			if( $(this).val() == '' ){
				$(this).remove();
			}
		});

		$('#smartSearchForm').submit();
	}

	function searchSmartCheck(){
		var keyword = $('#search_keyword1').val().trim();

		$('#search_keyword').val(keyword);
		$('#smart_keyword').val(keyword);

		$('#smartSearchForm').find('input').each(function(){
			if( $(this).val() == '' ){
				$(this).remove();
			}
		});

		document.getElementById('smartSearchForm').submit();
	}

	$(function(){
		$("#search_keyword1").keydown(function(key) {
			if (key.keyCode == 13) {
				searchSmartCheck();
			}
		});
	});

	function go_smartSearch(txt,type){
		var color_arr = new Array();
		var brand_arr = new Array();
		var size_arr = new Array();
		var neck_arr = new Array();
		var model_arr = new Array();
		var season_arr = new Array();
		var angam_arr = new Array();
		var theme_arr = new Array();
		var type_arr = new Array();
		var pmemo6_arr = new Array();
		var pmemo3_arr = new Array();
		var pmemo5_arr = new Array();
		var height_arr = new Array();
		var pmemo15_arr = new Array();
		var pmemo2_arr = new Array();
		var pmemo14_arr = new Array();
		var style_arr = new Array();
		var deli_arr = new Array();
		var chk = $(event.target).closest('li');
		var form = $('#smartSearchForm');

		// btn event
		$(chk).toggleClass('on');

		// color 값
		$('.colors .on').each(function() {
			var color = $(this).find('a').data("color");
			color_arr.push(color);
		});

		// brand 값
		$('.brand .on').each(function() {
			var brand = $(this).find('a').data("brand");
			brand_arr.push(brand);
		});

		// size 값
		$('.size_list .on').each(function() {
			var size = $(this).find('a').data("size");
			size_arr.push(size);
		});

		// 넥종류 값
		$('.necks .on').each(function() {
			var neck = $(this).find('a').data("neck");
			neck_arr.push(neck);
		});

		// model 값
		$('.models .on').each(function() {
			var model = $(this).find('a').data("model");
			model_arr.push(model);
		});

		// season 값
		$('.season .on').each(function() {
			var season = $(this).find('a').data("season");
			season_arr.push(season);
		});

		// 안감
		$('.angam .on').each(function() {
			var angam = $(this).find('a').data("pmemo");
			angam_arr.push(angam);
		});

		// 테마
		$('.theme .on').each(function() {
			var theme = $(this).find('a').data("theme");
			theme_arr.push(theme);
		});

		//타입
		$('.type .on').each(function() {
			var type = $(this).find('a').data("type");
			type_arr.push(type);
		});

		//주요소재

		$('.pmemo6 .on').each(function() {
			var pmemo6 = $(this).find('a').data("pmemo6");
			console.log(pmemo6);
			pmemo6_arr.push(pmemo6);
		});

		//비침
		$('.pmemo3 .on').each(function() {
			var pmemo3 = $(this).find('a').data("pmemo3");
			pmemo3_arr.push(pmemo3);
		});

		//기장
		$('.height .on').each(function() {
			var height = $(this).find('a').data("height");
			height_arr.push(height);
		});

		//두께
		$('.pmemo5 .on').each(function() {
			var pmemo5 = $(this).find('a').data("pmemo5");
			pmemo5_arr.push(pmemo5);
		});

		//핏
		$('.pmemo15 .on').each(function() {
			var pmemo15 = $(this).find('a').data("pmemo15");
			pmemo15_arr.push(pmemo15);
		});

		//신축성
		$('.pmemo2 .on').each(function() {
			var pmemo2 = $(this).find('a').data("pmemo2");
			pmemo2_arr.push(pmemo2);
		});

		//밴딩
		$('.pmemo14 .on').each(function() {
			var pmemo14 = $(this).find('a').data("pmemo14");
			pmemo14_arr.push(pmemo14);
		});

		//스타일
		$('.style .on').each(function() {
			var style = $(this).find('a').data("style");
			style_arr.push(style);
		});

		//배송
		$('.deli .on').each(function() {
			var deli = $(this).find('a').data("deli");
			deli_arr.push(deli);
		});

		$('input[name="search_color"]').val(color_arr);
		$('input[name="search_brand"]').val(brand_arr);
		$('input[name="search_size"]').val(size_arr);
		$('input[name="search_neck"]').val(neck_arr);
		$('input[name="search_model"]').val(model_arr);
		$('input[name="search_season"]').val(season_arr);
		$('input[name="search_angam"]').val(angam_arr);
		$('input[name="search_theme"]').val(theme_arr);
		$('input[name="search_type"]').val(type_arr);
		$('input[name="search_pmemo6"]').val(pmemo6_arr);
		$('input[name="search_pmemo3"]').val(pmemo3_arr);
		$('input[name="search_pmemo5"]').val(pmemo5_arr);
		$('input[name="search_height"]').val(height_arr);
		$('input[name="search_pmemo15"]').val(pmemo15_arr);
		$('input[name="search_pmemo2"]').val(pmemo2_arr);
		$('input[name="search_pmemo14"]').val(pmemo14_arr);
		$('input[name="search_style"]').val(style_arr);
		$('input[name="search_deli"]').val(deli_arr);

	}

	function search_subject_change(subject) {
		$('#search_subject_text').html(subject);
	}

	$(function(){
		$('.tab_smart li').click(function(){
			if( $(this).text() == '' ){

				return;
			}
			var eq = $(this).data('eq');
			$('.smartSearchMenu td').removeClass('on');
			$(this).addClass('on');

			$('.rangeBox').hide().eq(eq).show();
			// 검색카테고리 변경
			eq = eq == 'all' ? '':eq;
			$('#search_cate').val(eq);


		});

		switch("")
		{
			case '0101': search_subject_change('원피스 검색'); break;
			case '0109': search_subject_change('블라우스 검색'); break;
			case '0103': search_subject_change('아우터 검색'); break;
			case '0110': search_subject_change('니트 검색'); break;
			case '0111': search_subject_change('티셔츠 검색'); break;
			case '0107': search_subject_change('스커트 검색'); break;
			case '0108': search_subject_change('팬츠 검색'); break;
			case '0303':  search_subject_change('Shoes 검색'); break;
			case '': search_subject_change('통합 검색'); break;
		}
	});
	</script>

<!-- Facebook Pixel Code 2019-10-14 정주연 -->
<script>
function fbqSearchKeyword() {
  var uri = document.referrer;
  if (!uri) return;
  var link = document.createElement('a');
  link.setAttribute('href', uri);
  var pattern = new RegExp('[\\?&](?:utm_term|n_keyword|q|query)=([^&#]*)');
  var query = link.search.match(pattern);
  if (query && query.length > 0) {
    var keyword = query[1].replace(/\+/gi, "%20");
  }
  if (keyword) {
    fbq('trackCustom', 'SearchKeyword', {
      hostname: link.hostname,
      keyword: decodeURIComponent(keyword),
    });
}
}
fbqSearchKeyword();
</script>
<!-- Facebook Pixel Code 2019-10-14 정주연 -->

function search_tabover(no,onload = 'N') {
	$('.tab_smart > li > a').removeClass('active');
	$('.tab_smart > li[data-eq="'+no+'"] > a').addClass('active');

	$('.tabcnt').hide();
	if(onload!='Y'){
		$('.tabcnt li').removeClass('on');
		$('input[class=eleVal]').val('');
	}

	$('.tabcnt_smart_'+no).show();
	$('.tabcnt_type_'+no).show();
	$('.tabcnt_selfbrand_'+no).show();
	$('.tabcnt_brand_'+no).show();
	$('.tabcnt_pmemo6_'+no).show();
	$('.tabcnt_height_'+no).show();

	var sizeCate = ['all','0103','0110','0111','0101','0109','0107','0108','0601','2309'];
	if( sizeCate.indexOf(no) >= 0 ){
		$('.tabcnt_size').show();
	}else{
		$('.tabcnt_size').hide().find('li').removeClass('on');
	}
	var seasonCate = ['all','0103','0110','0111','0101','0109','0107','0108','0601','2309'];
	if( seasonCate.indexOf(no) >= 0 ){
		$('.tabcnt_season').show();
	}else{
		$('.tabcnt_season').hide().find('li').removeClass('on');
	}

	var pmemo3Cate = ['0103','0110','0111','0101','0109','0107','0108','0601','2309'];
	if( pmemo3Cate.indexOf(no) >= 0 ){
		$('.tabcnt_pmemo3').show();
	}else{
		$('.tabcnt_pmemo3').hide().find('li').removeClass('on');
	}

	var pmemo5Cate = ['0103','0110','0111','0101','0109','0107','0108','0601'];

<script>
	
// 프로모션 카운트다운
var promotionEndTime = '372408';
function countTime(seconds) {
	if(promotionEndTime < 1 || !promotionEndTime){
		clearInterval(promotioncount);
		$('.promotionEndTime').text("00:00:00");
		return;
	}
	var minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
	seconds %= 60;
	var hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
	minutes %= 60;
	var days = Math.floor(hours / 24);
	hours %= 24;
	hours = (hours+'') .padStart(2, '0');
	minutes = (minutes+'') .padStart(2, '0');
	seconds = (seconds+'') .padStart(2, '0');
	var daytext = days > 0 ? days+'일 ' : '';
	var daytext = '';
	$('.promotionEndTime').text(daytext+hours+" : "+minutes+" : "+seconds);
}

var promotioncount = setInterval(() => {
	countTime(promotionEndTime);
	promotionEndTime--;
}, 1000);

// gif 스크롤시 움직이게
var gifLib = {

	frames:'',
	load:()=>{ // 정지 이미지 구현
		gifLib.tracking();
	},

	ani:new IntersectionObserver((entries) => { // 트래킹 함수
		entries.forEach((entry) => {
			const target = entry.target;
			if (entry.isIntersecting){
				target.src = target.src;
			}
		});
	}),

	tracking:()=>{
		// document.querySelectorAll('.gifLibBox .prdimg a img:not(.gif-lib-track)').forEach(item=>{
		document.querySelectorAll('img[src*=\'.gif\']:not(.gif-lib-track)').forEach(item=>{
			item.classList.add('gif-lib-track');
			gifLib.ani.observe(item);
		});
	}
}
$("img[src*=\'.gif\'].gif-lib-track").removeClass('gif-lib-track');
gifLib.load();
$(window).ajaxSuccess( function(){ gifLib.load(); });

// Lazy Load Image Script
var lazyLib = {
	load: () => {
		lazyLib.tracking();
	},

	ani: new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
		entries.forEach(entry => {
			const target = entry.target;
			if (entry.isIntersecting) {

				// 이미지 처리
				if (target.tagName === 'IMG') {
					target.src = target.getAttribute('data-src');
				}
				// 비디오 소스 처리
				else if (target.tagName === 'VIDEO') {
					target.autoplay = true;   
					target.play().catch(err => {
						console.warn('Video play failed:', err);
					});  
					var swiperParent = target.closest('.swiper-container');
					if (swiperParent?.swiper) {
						swiperParent.swiper.update();
					}


				}
				lazyLib.ani.unobserve(target);
			}
		});
	}),

	tracking: () => {
		// 이미지 소스 로딩
		document.querySelectorAll('img[loading="lazy"][src=""][data-src]').forEach(img => {
			lazyLib.ani.observe(img);
		});
		// 비디오 소스 로딩
		document.querySelectorAll('video[loading="lazy"]:not([autoplay])').forEach(video => {
			lazyLib.ani.observe(video);
		});
	}
}

$(window).on('ajaxSuccess', function() {
	lazyLib.load();
});
lazyLib.load();

</script>
